With a 200-seed prompt library and an auditor-target-judge architecture, Petri provides reproducible safety assessments across multiple attack vectors.</p><div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Evaluation</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Adversarial</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">LLM Safety</span></div></article><article class=\"p-8 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-muted-dark/20 hover:bg-muted-dark/40 transition-colors duration-300\"><div class=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium bg-accent/10 text-accent rounded-full\">Architecture</span><span class=\"text-sm text-muted\">January 2025</span></div><h2 class=\"text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground mb-4 hover:text-accent transition-colors duration-200\"><a href=\"#\">Five-Layer Guard Agent Architecture for Autonomous Systems</a></h2><p class=\"text-muted leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-4xl\">We propose a five-layer architecture for securing autonomous AI agents: input validation, output filtering, memory integrity checks, tool call auditing, and behavioral drift detection. This architecture forms the foundation of our Trishool product.</p><div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Architecture</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Agentic Security</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Runtime Protection</span></div></article><article class=\"p-8 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-muted-dark/20 hover:bg-muted-dark/40 transition-colors duration-300\"><div class=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium bg-accent/10 text-accent rounded-full\">Alignment</span><span class=\"text-sm text-muted\">December 2024</span></div><h2 class=\"text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground mb-4 hover:text-accent transition-colors duration-200\"><a href=\"#\">Covenant72B: Constitutional Alignment at Scale</a></h2><p class=\"text-muted leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-4xl\">Covenant72B demonstrates that constitutional AI approaches can scale to 72 billion parameter models. We detail the training methodology, benchmark results, and lessons learned from aligning a large-scale model with safety principles.</p><div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Alignment</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Scale</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Constitutional AI</span></div></article><article class=\"p-8 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-muted-dark/20 hover:bg-muted-dark/40 transition-colors duration-300\"><div class=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium bg-accent/10 text-accent rounded-full\">Evaluation</span><span class=\"text-sm text-muted\">November 2024</span></div><h2 class=\"text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground mb-4 hover:text-accent transition-colors duration-200\"><a href=\"#\">Evaluating Guard Models Against Emerging Attack Vectors</a></h2><p class=\"text-muted leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-4xl\">A comprehensive evaluation of current guard models against newly discovered attack vectors. We identify vulnerabilities in existing solutions and propose improvements for next-generation safety classifiers.</p><div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Evaluation</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Guard Models</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Security</span></div></article><article class=\"p-8 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-muted-dark/20 hover:bg-muted-dark/40 transition-colors duration-300\"><div class=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium bg-accent/10 text-accent rounded-full\">Security</span><span class=\"text-sm text-muted\">October 2024</span></div><h2 class=\"text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground mb-4 hover:text-accent transition-colors duration-200\"><a href=\"#\">Runtime Monitoring for Agentic AI Systems</a></h2><p class=\"text-muted leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-4xl\">Techniques for monitoring AI agents in production to detect and prevent unsafe behavior. Our approach combines rule-based heuristics with learned anomaly detection for comprehensive runtime protection.</p><div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Security</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Runtime</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Monitoring</span></div></article></div></div></section><section class=\"py-24 md:py-32 border-t border-border mesh-gradient\"><div class=\"max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 text-center\"><h2 class=\"text-3xl md:text-4xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground mb-6\">Stay Updated</h2><p class=\"text-lg text-muted mb-10 max-w-2xl mx-auto\">Subscribe to our research newsletter to receive updates on new publications and findings.</p><a href=\"mailto:hello@astroware.ai?subject=Research%20Newsletter%20Subscription\" class=\"inline-flex items-center px-8 py-4 text-base font-medium bg-foreground text-background rounded-full hover:bg-muted-foreground transition-colors duration-200\">Subscribe</a></div></section></div><!--$--><!--/$--></main><footer class=\"border-t border-border bg-background\"><div class=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 md:py-16\"><div class=\"grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-8 lg:gap-12\"><div class=\"col-span-2 md:col-span-1\"><a class=\"inline-block\" href=\"/\"><span class=\"text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground\">Astroware</span></a><p class=\"mt-4 text-sm text-muted leading-relaxed max-w-xs\">AI Alignment &amp; Security Research Lab. We detail the training methodology, benchmark results, and lessons learned from aligning a large-scale model with safety principles.</p><div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Alignment</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Scale</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Constitutional AI</span></div></article><article class=\"p-8 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-muted-dark/20 hover:bg-muted-dark/40 transition-colors duration-300\"><div class=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium bg-accent/10 text-accent rounded-full\">Evaluation</span><span class=\"text-sm text-muted\">November 2024</span></div><h2 class=\"text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground mb-4 hover:text-accent transition-colors duration-200\"><a href=\"#\">Evaluating Guard Models Against Emerging Attack Vectors</a></h2><p class=\"text-muted leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-4xl\">A comprehensive evaluation of current guard models against newly discovered attack vectors. We identify vulnerabilities in existing solutions and propose improvements for next-generation safety classifiers.</p><div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Evaluation</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Guard Models</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Security</span></div></article><article class=\"p-8 rounded-2xl border border-border bg-muted-dark/20 hover:bg-muted-dark/40 transition-colors duration-300\"><div class=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium bg-accent/10 text-accent rounded-full\">Security</span><span class=\"text-sm text-muted\">October 2024</span></div><h2 class=\"text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground mb-4 hover:text-accent transition-colors duration-200\"><a href=\"#\">Runtime Monitoring for Agentic AI Systems</a></h2><p class=\"text-muted leading-relaxed mb-6 max-w-4xl\">Techniques for monitoring AI agents in production to detect and prevent unsafe behavior. Our approach combines rule-based heuristics with learned anomaly detection for comprehensive runtime protection.</p><div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\"><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Security</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Runtime</span><span class=\"px-3 py-1 text-xs bg-muted-dark text-muted-foreground rounded-full\">Monitoring</span></div></article></div></div></section><section class=\"py-24 md:py-32 border-t border-border mesh-gradient\"><div class=\"max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 text-center\"><h2 class=\"text-3xl md:text-4xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground mb-6\">Stay Updated</h2><p class=\"text-lg text-muted mb-10 max-w-2xl mx-auto\">Subscribe to our research newsletter to receive updates on new publications and findings.</p><a href=\"mailto:hello@astroware.ai?subject=Research%20Newsletter%20Subscription\" class=\"inline-flex items-center px-8 py-4 text-base font-medium bg-foreground text-background rounded-full hover:bg-muted-foreground transition-colors duration-200\">Subscribe</a></div></section></div><!--$--><!--/$--></main><footer class=\"border-t border-border bg-background\"><div class=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12 md:py-16\"><div class=\"grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-8 lg:gap-12\"><div class=\"col-span-2 md:col-span-1\"><a class=\"inline-block\" href=\"/\"><span class=\"text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground\">Astroware</span></a><p class=\"mt-4 text-sm text-muted leading-relaxed max-w-xs\">AI Alignment &amp; Security Research Lab.
